message = 'mismatched arrival signature'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        ("variant", "owner_scopes", "message"),
        [
            ("legacy_two_scopes", (), "synchronizes exactly one scope"),
            ("three_owners", ("owner0", "owner1", "owner2"), "exactly two ordered owner scopes"),
            ("different_order", ("owner0", "owner1"), "declared order"),
            ("external", ("owner0", "owner1"), "outside both declared owners"),
            ("guarded", ("owner0", "owner1"), "guarded owner-ring epochs"),
            ("read_first", ("owner0", "owner1"), "read-first owner-ring epoch"),
            ("stage_offsets", ("owner0", "owner1"), "stage-zero epochs"),
            ("mismatched_arrivals", ("owner0", "owner1"), "mismatched arrival signature"),
            ("private", ("owner0", "owner1"), "CTA-local shared storage"),
            ("global", ("owner0", "owner1"), "CTA-local shared storage"),
            ("cross_cta", ("owner0", "owner1"), "CTA-local shared storage"),
            ("nested", ("owner0", "owner1"), "ordered siblings"),
            ("async", ("owner0", "owner1"), "asynchronous atom"),
            ("three_roles", ("owner0", "owner1"), "exactly two fixed nonoverlapping warp roles"),
        ],
    )
    def test_unsupported_owner_ring_cases_rejected(variant, owner_scopes, message):
        owners = () if variant == "legacy_two_scopes" else owner_scopes

    def test_stage_order_materializes_scheduled_bind_clones():
        tir = tvm.tirx
        a = tir.decl_buffer((8,), "int32", name="bind_A")
        b = tir.decl_buffer((8,), "int32", name="bind_B")
        k = tir.Var("k", "int32")
        value = tir.Var("scheduled_value", "int32")
        statements = [
            tir.BufferStore(a, 1, [k]),
            tir.Bind(value, tir.BufferLoad(a, [k]) + 1),
            tir.BufferStore(b, value, [k]),
        ]
        annotations = _annotations(latencies=[1, 1, 1], iis=[1, 1, 1], resource_flags=[0, 0, 0], stage_order_deps=[(1, 2)])
        annotations["num_stages"] = tir.IntImm("int32", 2)
        loop = tir.For(k, 0, 4, tir.ForKind.SERIAL, tir.SeqStmt(statements), annotations=annotations)
        func = tir.PrimFunc([a.data, b.data], loop, buffer_map={a.data: a, b.data: b}).with_attr("global_symbol", "main")
        def solver_result(call):
            starts_by_tasks = {3: [1, 2, 5], 4: [1, 2, 5, 4]}
            return starts_by_tasks[len(call["latencies"])], [], 4
    
        calls = _install_solver(solver_result)
        planned = _run_z3(func)
        loop = _find_loop(planned["main"])
        assert len(calls) == 2
        plan = _clone_plan(loop)
        assert len(plan) == 1
        assert int(plan[0]["definition_task"]) == 1
        assert int(plan[0]["consumer_task"]) == 2
        assert _ints(loop.annotations["tl_z3_pipeline_audit_starts"]) == [1, 2, 5]
    
        # Planner support is complete, but fresh Vars and consumer rewriting still
        # have to be materialized by InjectSoftwarePipeline. Keep that integration
        # contract visible rather than silently treating metadata as cloned IR.
        with tvm.transform.PassContext(config={_CONFIG: True}):
            stock = tl.transform.PipelinePlanning()(planned)
            stock_loop = _find_loop(stock["main"])
            assert _ints(stock_loop.annotations["software_pipeline_replayable_scalar_binds"]) == [0, 0, 0]

    def test_main_cuda_pipeline_infers_before_z3_planning(tmp_path):
        hardware, calibration = _write_specs(tmp_path)
        annotations = {
            "tl_z3_pipeline": True,
            "tl_z3_pipeline_hardware": str(hardware),
            "tl_z3_pipeline_calibration": str(calibration),
            "tl_z3_pipeline_manual_schedule": True,
            "tl_z3_pipeline_manual_stages": _imms([0, 1]),
            "tl_z3_pipeline_enable_offset": True,
        }
    
        @T.prim_func
        def main(A: T.Tensor((64,), "float32"), B: T.Tensor((64,), "float32")):
            with T.Kernel(1, threads=128):
                a_shared = T.alloc_shared((16,), "float32")
                for k in T.Pipelined(4, num_stages=2, annotations=annotations):
                    T.copy(A[k * 16 : (k + 1) * 16], a_shared, coalesced_width=16)
                    for i in T.Parallel(16):
                        B[k * 16 + i] = a_shared[i] + 1.0
    
        from tilelang.cuda.pipeline import CUDAPassPipelineBodyPrologue
    
        events = []
        estimator = tvm_ffi.get_global_func(_ESTIMATOR)
        solver = tvm_ffi.get_global_func(_SOLVER)
    
        def tracked_estimator(*args):
            events.append("estimate")
            return estimator(*args)
    
        def tracked_solver(*args):
            events.append("solve")
            return solver(*args)
    
        tvm_ffi.register_global_func(_ESTIMATOR, f=tracked_estimator, override=True)
        tvm_ffi.register_global_func(_SOLVER, f=tracked_solver, override=True)

    def CUDAPassPipelineBodyPrologue(mod: IRModule, target: Target) -> IRModule:
        pass_ctx = tilelang.transform.get_pass_context()
        z3_pipeline_enabled = pass_ctx.config.get(_Z3_PIPELINE_CONFIG, False)
        z3_task_analysis_enabled = pass_ctx.config.get(_Z3_TASK_ANALYSIS_CONFIG, False)
        has_z3_annotations = (z3_pipeline_enabled or z3_task_analysis_enabled) and _module_has_z3_pipeline_annotations(mod)
        z3_pipeline_module = bool(z3_pipeline_enabled) and has_z3_annotations
        if z3_task_analysis_enabled and has_z3_annotations and not z3_pipeline_enabled:
            raise ValueError(
                "CUDA Z3 task analysis requires tl.enable_z3_pipeline_planning in the main CUDA pipeline. "
                "For offline annotation inspection, call InferZ3PipelineProblem directly without stock planning."
            )
        if (
            z3_pipeline_module
            and _module_has_existing_warp_specialization(mod)
            and not _module_has_z3_owner_ring_annotations(mod)
        ):
            raise ValueError("CUDA Z3 pipeline planning cannot overwrite an existing warp-specialization schedule")
        if z3_pipeline_module and pass_ctx.config.get(_AUTO_WS_CONFIG) is not None:
            raise ValueError(
                "CUDA Z3 pipeline planning and automatic warp specialization cannot "
                "schedule the same annotated module. Disable "
                "tl.enable_auto_warp_specialization or tl.enable_z3_pipeline_planning."
            )
    
        mod = tirx.transform.BindTarget(target)(mod)
        mod = tilelang.transform.MaterializeKernelLaunch(lower_thread_binding=True, default_threads=128)(mod)
        # Record body-bound global bases before optional let inlining obscures
        # their provenance. CopyAnalysis consumes this marker for every lowering
        # path, independently of whether warp specialization is enabled.
        mod = tilelang.cuda.transform.AnnotateDeviceBoundTmaCopies()(mod)
        if should_force_let_inline():
            # Force-let inline whenever the pass config requests it.
            mod = tilelang.transform.LetInline()(mod)
        # Add wrapper for single buf store
        mod = tilelang.transform.AddWrapperForSingleBufStore()(mod)
        # Normalize negative indices to canonical non-negative form
        mod = tilelang.transform.LegalizeNegativeIndex()(mod)
        # Verify parallel loop correctness
        if should_enable_race_check():
            mod = tilelang.transform.VerifyParallelLoop()(mod)
        # Inject assumes to speedup tvm prover
        mod = tilelang.transform.InjectAssumes()(mod)
        # Simplify the IR expressions
        mod = tilelang.transform.Simplify()(mod)
        # Verify reducer v2 epoch lifecycle and access rules (early, so
        # diagnostics point at user-written code)
        mod = tilelang.transform.CanonicalizeLegacyReducer()(mod)
        mod = tilelang.transform.VerifyReducerEpoch()(mod)
        # Warn on buffers that are read before anything writes them.
        # Runs after the reducer passes above, so legacy reducers have been
        # canonicalized, and before PipelinePlanning and LowerTileOp, while
        # loop bodies are still in source order and tile ops still declare
        # their access regions.
        mod = tilelang.transform.VerifyBufferInit()(mod)
    
        # @CUDA-specific
        # Tile-level warp specialization: runs before layout inference so that
        # producer/consumer split happens at the high-level tile-op IR.
        # AutoWarpSpecialization: derive a WSSchedule for kernels without one, using the scheduler named by tl.enable_auto_warp_specialization (opt-in; no-op when unset).
        # MaterializeWSSchedule: Materialize a user-provided warp-specialization schedule (T.annotate_ws_schedule).
        # ProducerConsumerWarpSpecialized: The pass classifies copy ops as TMA/cp.async/sync inline. Shared buffers are multi-versioned internally only for functions where the WS transformation actually applies.
        # G3 owns annotated modules before this legacy/automatic WS rewrite point.
        # In particular, ProducerConsumerWarpSpecialized can consume num_stages even
        # when no auto-scheduler is named, so it must not rewrite a G3 loop first.
        if allow_warp_specialized(target=target) and not z3_pipeline_module:
            mod = tilelang.cuda.transform.AutoWarpSpecialization()(mod)
            mod = tilelang.cuda.transform.MaterializeWSSchedule()(mod)
            mod = tilelang.cuda.transform.ProducerConsumerWarpSpecialized()(mod)
    
        # @CUDA / Blackwell specific
        # Lower 2SM TCGEN5MMA and related on Blackwell target (must run before
        # LayoutInference so that the use_2cta annotation is visible to infer_layout)
        mod = tilelang.cuda.transform.LowerBlackwell2SM()(mod)
    
        # Normalize if-without-else wrappers before pipeline planning. This keeps
        # pipeline body extraction focused on canonical SeqStmt bodies.
        mod = tilelang.transform.IfStmtBinding()(mod)
    
        # Expand only explicitly requested unroll loops before pipeline planning
        # and fold the constants exposed by substitution. This makes their
        # copy/compute statements individual scheduling units.
        mod = tilelang.transform.UnrollLoop()(mod)
        # Simplify the unrolled loop bodies.
        mod = tilelang.transform.Simplify()(mod)
    
        # Infer after unroll/Simplify so analysis and planning see the same tasks.
        # Both passes are opt-in; the stock CUDA pass sequence is unchanged by default.
        if z3_task_analysis_enabled and has_z3_annotations:
            mod = tilelang.transform.InferZ3PipelineProblem()(mod)
        if z3_pipeline_enabled:

    def test_async_pipeline_preserves_four_task_z3_shape():
        @T.prim_func
        def before(
            A: T.Tensor((16, 12), T.float32),
            B: T.Tensor((16, 12), T.float32),
            C: T.Tensor((16, 12), T.float32),
        ):
            for tx in T.thread_binding(0, 16, thread="threadIdx.x"):
                a_shared = T.alloc_buffer((16, 1), dtype=T.float32, scope="shared")
                b_shared = T.alloc_buffer((16, 1), dtype=T.float32, scope="shared")
                c_fragment = T.alloc_buffer((16, 1), dtype=T.float32, scope="local.fragment")
                for i in T.serial(
                    0,
                    12,
                    annotations={
                        # starts=[1, 2, 6, 8], II=2, normalized to stages 0/1/3/4.
                        "software_pipeline_stage": [0, 1, 3, 4],
                        "software_pipeline_order": [1, 0, 2, 3],
                        "software_pipeline_async_stages": [0, 1],
                        "software_pipeline_async_producers": [1, 1, 0, 0],
                        "software_pipeline_async_producer_groups": [0, 0, -1, -1],
                        "num_stages": 5,
                    },
                ):
                    with T.sblock("copy_a"):
                        T.reads(A[tx, i])
                        T.writes(a_shared[tx, 0])
                        a_shared[tx, 0] = A[tx, i]
                    with T.sblock("copy_b"):
                        T.reads(B[tx, i])
                        T.writes(b_shared[tx, 0])
                        b_shared[tx, 0] = B[tx, i]
                    with T.sblock("consume"):
                        T.reads(a_shared[tx, 0], b_shared[tx, 0])
                        T.writes(c_fragment[tx, 0])
                        c_fragment[tx, 0] = a_shared[tx, 0] + T.float32(2) * b_shared[tx, 0]
                    with T.sblock("output"):
                        T.reads(c_fragment[tx, 0])
                        T.writes(C[tx, i])
                        C[tx, i] = c_fragment[tx, 0]
